Ligne 1 : A=Store name as per Uber Eats manager | B=External store ID as per Uber Eats manager | C=Order ID as per Uber Eats manager | D=Unique ID to identify the order  | E=Local date the order was placed, or local date of the original order placed for which there is a refund | F=Local timestamp for when order was accepted by the merchant  | G=The mode of order fulfillment whether it was delivery by courier via Uber network, delivery by merchant, Pick-up by customer or Dine-in | H=Mode of payment used for order payment | I=The platform from which the customer ordered, (i.e. iOS, Android, Uber Eats Web) | J=Either: Completed (eater received food), Cancelled (order cancelled by eater or support), Refund (eater was refunded for order), or Unfulfilled (order was not able to be completed) | K=Uber membership status of the customer who placed the order | L=Total item sales excl tax  | M=Tax on total item sales in the order | N=Amount merchants are responsible for refunding customers when they report order errors (excl tax) | O=Tax Refund On Sales | P=Adjustments merchants made to the price of items once an order has been placed (excl tax) | Q=Tax on in-store price adjustments | R=Goods and Sales Tax / Harmonized Sales Tax on item Sales | S=Provincial Sales Tax on item Sales (where applicable) as per the tax laws applicable  | T=Quebec Sales Tax on item Sales (applicable in Quebec) as per the tax laws applicable  | U=Retail Sales Tax on item Sales (applicable in Manitoba) as per the tax laws applicable  | V=Merchant promotions applied to the order | W=Tax on merchant promotions applied to the order | X=Merchant promotions applied to the delivery fee charged to Eaters | Y=Tax on merchant promotions applied to the delivery fee charged to Eaters | Z=Amount merchant charges Uber for co-funded marketing campaigns. Varies depending on co-funding % for the campaign. | AA=Amount commercially agreed to between Merchant and Uber added on a per item basis and charged to the Eater on Merchant’s behalf	 | AB=Applicable GST/HST due on the Markup Amount | AC=Applicable QST due on the Markup Amount | AD=Applicable PST due on the Markup Amount | AE=Total item sales including promotions, adjustments and bag fee incl tax where applicable | AF=Fee charged by Merchants to Eaters for delivery (excl. tax). Only relevant for self delivery orders. | AG=Tax on delivery fee. Only relevant for self delivery orders | AH=Goods and Sales Tax / Harmonized Sales Tax on Delivery Fee  Only relevant for self delivery orders | AI=Provincial Sales Tax on Delivery Fee (where applicable)  Only relevant for self delivery orders | AJ=Quebec Sales Tax on Delivery Fee (applicable in Quebec)  Only relevant for self delivery orders | AK=Retail Sales Tax on Delivery Fee (applicable in Manitoba)  Only relevant for self delivery orders | AL=Fee charged to eater by the merchant for the packaging material | AM=Total item sales including promotions and adjustments, bag fee and the delivery fee (self-delivery order) incl. tax where applicable | AN=Cost of delivery excl tax (i.e. courier fare)  | AO=Tax on courier fare (where applicable) | AP=Goods and Sales Tax / Harmonized Sales Tax on Cost of Delivery | AQ=Provincial Sales Tax on Cost of Delivery (where applicable) | AR=Quebec Sales Tax on Cost of Delivery (applicable in Quebec) | AS=Retail Sales Tax on Cost of Delivery (applicable in Manitoba) | AT=Cost of delivery (i.e. courier fare) (including tax) | AU=The fee Uber charges to merchant to make their store visible to potential customers, provide support and other related services ( %fee is applied to sub-total before discounts)  | AV=Discount applied to Uber's service fee. Occurs when cost of delivery is greater than the delivery fee. (where applicable) | AW=Total Tax on Uber Service Fee charged to merchant | AX=Goods and Sales Tax / Harmonized Sales Tax on Uber Service Fee charged to merchant | AY=Provincial Sales Tax on Uber Service Fee charged to merchant | AZ=Quebec Sales Tax on Uber Service Fee charged to merchant (applicable in Quebec) | BA=Retail Sales Tax on Uber Service Fee charged to merchant (applicable in Manitoba) | BB=Additional consideration Uber charges Merchant equal to the amount of Markup. | BC=Applicable GST/HST due on the Service Fee (Markup). | BD=Applicable QST due on the Service Fee (Markup). | BE=Applicable PST due on the Service Fee (Markup). | BF=Goods and Sales Tax / Harmonized Sales Tax on Advertising fee charged by Uber to merchant  | BG=Quebec Sales Tax on Advertising fee charged by Uber to merchant (applicable in Quebec) | BH=Provincial Sales Tax on Advertising fee charged by Uber to merchant | BI=Goods and Sales Tax / Harmonized Sales Tax on Advertising credit provided by Uber to merchant  | BJ=Quebec Sales Tax on Advertising credit provided by Uber to merchant (applicable in Quebec) | BK=Provincial Sales Tax on Advertising credit provided by Uber to merchant | BL=Where delivery fee is greater than cost of delivery, this is equal to (delivery fee minus the cost of delivery) where applicable | BM=For merchants who perform their own delivery, the tip from the Eater to the courier | BN=Description of adhoc one-time payments or charges from Uber to the merchant | BO=All miscellaneous payments like Ad payments, Ad credits etc. inclusive of tax | BP=Sales tax Uber Eats is required to pay in jurisdictions with marketplace facilitator laws  | BQ=Total payout associated with this order (negative if associated with a refund)  Sales + Adjustments - promotions + marketing adjustment + Bag fee + delivery fee (self delivery) + tips (self-delivery) - marketplace fee +/- Other payments - marketplace facilitator tax  (incl tax where applicable) | BR=Date payout initiated by Uber (generally the Monday following the prior week) | BS=Either: Paid (payment has been sent to bank), Pending (payment has been created in our system), Rejected by Bank (the receiving bank account has not accepted the payment) | BT=Link to the 'Uber to Restaurant' invoice .pdf | BU=Link to the 'Courier to Restaurant' invoice .pdf | BV=Link to the 'Restaurant to Eater' invoice .pdf | BW=This is the ID from retailers, used to identify retailer loyalty customers | BX=The reference ID for identifying the payout related to this order in the Payout Summary Report CSV

Provenance et contrôle des pièces

Les originaux, les déclarations et les analyses sont identifiés par leur provenance. Les doublons et les versions d’une facture ne s’additionnent pas. Une extraction OCR reste à contrôler sur la pièce originale. Les documents à obtenir et les vérifications ouvertes figurent dans les synthèses de chaque dossier.

Les pièces d’origine restent dans leurs dossiers. L’index est une copie de travail datée; une recherche sans résultat ne démontre pas l’absence d’une pièce. Les synchronisations OneDrive et GitHub sont distinctes.
